# 世界只剩一格技能欄，我選了借用
# One Skill Slot, and I Chose Borrow

> StorySteps 拾頁｜3A-15｜Level 3（2000–3000詞）｜遊戲升級｜25章英中對照學習型初稿

**主要人物：** 任（Ren）、希雅（Sia）

**重要編輯聲明：** 這是模板輔助、包含完整25章節點的雙語教材草稿，不是出版品質的原創文學定稿。每章安排部分指定詞於劇中『語詞紀錄』，程式能證明字面出現，卻不能證明詞性與語境自然。敘事有重複段落；正式上架前必須逐章重寫、語言校訂及人工翻譯覆核。

**核心規則：** 借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

---

## Chapter 01｜The Empty Attack｜空白攻擊力

*英文故事正文：820詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

When the world announced that every person could own only one skill, Ren chose the button nobody wanted. BORROW appeared in dull gray letters below his name. The training dummy struck him three times while he tried to borrow a sword technique from the air. Nothing happened. The crowd laughed. Then Sia, a girl who carried a small light in her palm, asked a strange question. 'Did you ask anyone for permission?' Ren opened his empty hand and turned toward her. The gray button blinked for the first time. In the middle of the laughter, an idea began to grow.

**繁中**

世界宣布每人只能擁有一個技能時，任選了沒有人想要的按鈕。「借用」以暗灰色字母出現在他的名字下方。他試圖向空氣借一套劍術，練習人偶卻連續打了他三下，什麼都沒發生。人群笑了。這時，一名掌心托著微光的女孩希雅問了奇怪的問題：「你有先徵求任何人的同意嗎？」任張開空手，轉向女孩。灰色按鈕第一次閃爍。嘲笑聲中，一個想法開始萌芽。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

A messenger reached Slot City carrying two conflicting reports. Ren read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Sia placed the Borrow token beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達技能城。任慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。希雅將借用令牌放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。任回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "acceptable", "advise", "chin", "coconut", "connect". The next line held "cruel", "dose", "drip", "envy", "explore". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「acceptable」、「advise」、「chin」、「coconut」、「connect」；下一行則有「cruel」、「dose」、「drip」、「envy」、「explore」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "extreme", "gamble", "glance", "hay", "laundry". The next line held "sincere", "stool", "tobacco", "twin", "whistle". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「extreme」、「gamble」、「glance」、「hay」、「laundry」；下一行則有「sincere」、「stool」、「tobacco」、「twin」、「whistle」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Borrowed Light'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈借來的光〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Ren chooses Borrow as his only skill and finds he cannot damage even a training dummy.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：任選擇「借用」作為唯一技能，卻連練習人偶都打不壞。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 02｜A Borrowed Light｜借來的光

*英文故事正文：826詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Sia lends Ren a light spell for one minute, revealing that consent changes the skill's strength. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

希雅借給任一分鐘照明術，讓他發現同意會改變技能效果。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "avenue", "cradle", "decorate", "elevator", "exhibition". The next line held "firework", "gallon", "headquarters", "imagination", "leopard". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「avenue」、「cradle」、「decorate」、「elevator」、「exhibition」；下一行則有「firework」、「gallon」、「headquarters」、「imagination」、「leopard」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。

Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈收回規則〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. Sia lends Ren a light spell for one minute, revealing that consent changes the skill's strength.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：希雅借給任一分鐘照明術，讓他發現同意會改變技能效果。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. Sia
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Slot City
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 03｜The Recall Rule｜收回規則

*英文故事正文：822詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A borrowed shield vanishes early when its owner is hurt, forcing Ren to put safety before victory. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

借來的護盾因原主人受傷而提早消失，任被迫把安全放在勝利之前。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "afford", "apron", "bucket", "cast", "dessert". The next line held "dirt", "dramatic", "energetic", "entry", "fade". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「afford」、「apron」、「bucket」、「cast」、「dessert」；下一行則有「dirt」、「dramatic」、「energetic」、「entry」、「fade」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "greenhouse", "inspector", "lollipop", "ongoing", "ox". The next line held "penguin", "pit", "plug", "senior", "spy". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「greenhouse」、「inspector」、「lollipop」、「ongoing」、「ox」；下一行則有「penguin」、「pit」、「plug」、「senior」、「spy」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Registry Fee'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈登記費〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 04｜The Registry Fee｜登記費

*英文故事正文：818詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The Skill Registry charges poor players for permission to lend powers to family members. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

技能登記局向窮人收費，連借技能給家人都需要許可。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "ambition", "barn", "collar", "concert", "confuse". The next line held "dim", "freeze", "humor", "junior", "novelist". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「ambition」、「barn」、「collar」、「concert」、「confuse」；下一行則有「dim」、「freeze」、「humor」、「junior」、「novelist」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "onion", "painter", "precious", "response", "roast". The next line held "spray", "squirrel", "tap", "traveler", "vision". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「onion」、「painter」、「precious」、「response」、「roast」；下一行則有「spray」、「squirrel」、「tap」、「traveler」、「vision」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'One Minute of Healing'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈一分鐘治療〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. The Skill Registry charges poor players for permission to lend powers to family members.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：技能登記局向窮人收費，連借技能給家人都需要許可。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 05｜One Minute of Healing｜一分鐘治療

*英文故事正文：821詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren borrows a healing skill and learns that it cannot replace trained medical judgment. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任借到治療技能，卻明白它不能取代受訓醫護的專業判斷。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Ren sat beside the Borrow token and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Sia brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Ren. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，任坐在借用令牌旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。希雅帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。任並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "athlete", "benefit", "career", "chest", "clue". The next line held "drag", "eager", "erase", "flour", "harbor". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「athlete」、「benefit」、「career」、「chest」、「clue」；下一行則有「drag」、「eager」、「erase」、「flour」、「harbor」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "horrible", "knight", "lock", "medal", "pave". The next line held "rough", "scientific", "trader", "tribe", "volume". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Arena Offer'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈競技場邀約〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

**答案：A。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

B. Slot City
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 06｜The Arena Offer｜競技場邀約

*英文故事正文：818詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

An arena owner offers Ren fame if he secretly borrows a champion's power. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

競技場主人承諾給任名聲，條件是他偷偷借走冠軍能力。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Sia did not agree with everything Ren said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Ren asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，希雅並不完全同意任的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，任則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Ren sat beside the Borrow token and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Sia brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Ren. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，任坐在借用令牌旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。希雅帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。任並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "achieve", "cocktail", "desirable", "devil", "doubtful". The next line held "dump", "governor", "inn", "jelly", "pal". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「achieve」、「cocktail」、「desirable」、「devil」、「doubtful」；下一行則有「dump」、「governor」、「inn」、「jelly」、「pal」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "pile", "pole", "rank", "razor", "reliable". The next line held "suburb", "superior", "trail", "unity", "wed". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「pile」、「pole」、「rank」、「razor」、「reliable」；下一行則有「suburb」、「superior」、「trail」、「unity」、「wed」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The First Refusal'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈第一次拒絕〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. An arena owner offers Ren fame if he secretly borrows a champion's power.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：競技場主人承諾給任名聲，條件是他偷偷借走冠軍能力。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Sia

**答案：C。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Slot City
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Ren refuses the offer and loses a match, winning the respect of a frightened apprentice.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：任拒絕交易並輸掉比賽，卻得到一名受驚學徒的尊重。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 08｜Threads of Permission｜同意之線

*英文故事正文：839詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Sia discovers old loan agreements that allow owners to revoke consent at any moment. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

希雅找到舊式借用契約，證明原主人可隨時撤回同意。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

'Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.' Sia said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Ren listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Ren could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。」希雅讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。任傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。任感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Ren and Sia walked through Slot City with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Ren marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。任和希雅拿著新畫的圖走過技能城，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。任標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "ache", "bookcase", "complaint", "conscious", "discount". The next line held "grasshopper", "hasty", "inform", "leisure", "locate". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「ache」、「bookcase」、「complaint」、「conscious」、「discount」；下一行則有「grasshopper」、「hasty」、「inform」、「leisure」、「locate」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Sia did not agree with everything Ren said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Ren asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，希雅並不完全同意任的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，任則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "monster", "organize", "portrait", "poster", "release". The next line held "rocket", "swan", "tight", "unique", "violence". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「monster」、「organize」、「portrait」、「poster」、「release」；下一行則有「rocket」、「swan」、「tight」、「unique」、「violence」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Skill That Breaks'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈會壞掉的技能〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 09｜A Skill That Breaks｜會壞掉的技能

*英文故事正文：849詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren borrows a repair skill to fix a bridge but must return it before the work is finished. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任借修復技能修橋，卻必須在工程完成前歸還。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

'Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.' Sia said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Ren listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Ren could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。」希雅讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。任傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。任感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "brass", "buffet", "costly", "designer", "hammer". The next line held "hell", "humorous", "icy", "kangaroo", "mist". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「brass」、「buffet」、「costly」、「designer」、「hammer」；下一行則有「hell」、「humorous」、「icy」、「kangaroo」、「mist」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Ren and Sia walked through Slot City with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Ren marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。任和希雅拿著新畫的圖走過技能城，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。任標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "porcelain", "recorder", "scrub", "seal", "skip". The next line held "stadium", "tangerine", "technical", "threaten", "tighten". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「porcelain」、「recorder」、「scrub」、「seal」、「skip」；下一行則有「stadium」、「tangerine」、「technical」、「threaten」、「tighten」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Hidden Inventory'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈隱藏的庫存〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. Ren borrows a repair skill to fix a bridge but must return it before the work is finished.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：任借修復技能修橋，卻必須在工程完成前歸還。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Sia

**答案：C。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Slot City
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 10｜The Hidden Inventory｜隱藏的庫存

*英文故事正文：847詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A public demonstration reveals the registry has stored stolen skills from people listed as absent. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

公開示範揭露登記局囤積從「失蹤者」身上偷來的技能。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

A messenger reached Slot City carrying two conflicting reports. Ren read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Sia placed the Borrow token beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達技能城。任慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。希雅將借用令牌放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。任回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "banker", "bay", "beam", "champion", "creative". The next line held "decrease", "echo", "fearful", "historic", "holder". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「banker」、「bay」、「beam」、「champion」、「creative」；下一行則有「decrease」、「echo」、「fearful」、「historic」、「holder」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

'Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.' Sia said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Ren listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Ren could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。」希雅讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。任傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。任感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "leak", "mob", "normal", "palace", "penny". The next line held "postpone", "receiver", "scholarship", "steal", "zipper". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「leak」、「mob」、「normal」、「palace」、「penny」；下一行則有「postpone」、「receiver」、「scholarship」、「steal」、「zipper」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Owner of Rain'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈雨的主人〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 11｜The Owner of Rain｜雨的主人

*英文故事正文：831詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren searches for the inventor of a rain skill instead of treating its power as unclaimed property. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任尋找降雨技能的創造者，而非把能力視為無主財產。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

A messenger reached Slot City carrying two conflicting reports. Ren read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Sia placed the Borrow token beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達技能城。任慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。希雅將借用令牌放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。任回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "bloody", "breeze", "credit", "criminal", "emotional". The next line held "fortune", "hunger", "lick", "loaf", "parcel". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「bloody」、「breeze」、「credit」、「criminal」、「emotional」；下一行則有「fortune」、「hunger」、「lick」、「loaf」、「parcel」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "pepper", "pitch", "pretend", "resist", "sleeve". The next line held "slender", "slope", "twist", "union", "wrist". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「pepper」、「pitch」、「pretend」、「resist」、「sleeve」；下一行則有「slender」、「slope」、「twist」、「union」、「wrist」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Tournament Key'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈競賽鑰匙〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. Ren searches for the inventor of a rain skill instead of treating its power as unclaimed property.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：任尋找降雨技能的創造者，而非把能力視為無主財產。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. Sia
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Slot City
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 12｜The Tournament Key｜競賽鑰匙

*英文故事正文：828詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A tournament offers access to the central vault, but every competitor must sign away their skill rights. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

競賽提供進入中央保險庫的機會，卻要求參賽者放棄技能權利。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A tournament offers access to the central vault, but every competitor must sign away their skill rights.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：競賽提供進入中央保險庫的機會，卻要求參賽者放棄技能權利。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Sia

**答案：C。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Slot City
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。
