## Chapter 13｜The Two-Person Trial｜雙人試煉

*英文故事正文：822詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren and Sia enter as a team, solving puzzles that require one person to trust the other. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任與希雅組隊參賽，解開必須互相信任才能完成的謎題。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "amaze", "anxious", "bud", "charm", "cotton". The next line held "crown", "dairy", "element", "grocery", "holy". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「amaze」、「anxious」、「bud」、「charm」、「cotton」；下一行則有「crown」、「dairy」、「element」、「grocery」、「holy」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "hourly", "mess", "oak", "ownership", "pineapple". The next line held "representative", "scarf", "sip", "swear", "tag". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「hourly」、「mess」、「oak」、「ownership」、「pineapple」；下一行則有「representative」、「scarf」、「sip」、「swear」、「tag」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Borrowed Voice'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈借來的聲音〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 14｜A Borrowed Voice｜借來的聲音

*英文故事正文：819詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A mute witness lends Ren a voice skill so the stolen records can be read aloud. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

無法說話的證人借任聲音技能，讓被偷的紀錄得以公開。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "breathe", "bunch", "campus", "communicate", "desire". The next line held "electronic", "guidance", "headline", "inferior", "luggage". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「breathe」、「bunch」、「campus」、「communicate」、「desire」；下一行則有「electronic」、「guidance」、「headline」、「inferior」、「luggage」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "mall", "march", "native", "passport", "saving". The next line held "skinny", "stir", "trunk", "tub", "volleyball". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「mall」、「march」、「native」、「passport」、「saving」；下一行則有「skinny」、「stir」、「trunk」、「tub」、「volleyball」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Champion's Choice'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈冠軍的選擇〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. A mute witness lends Ren a voice skill so the stolen records can be read aloud.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：無法說話的證人借任聲音技能，讓被偷的紀錄得以公開。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. Sia
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Slot City
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 15｜The Champion's Choice｜冠軍的選擇

*英文故事正文：824詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The undefeated champion offers help freely after discovering the registry used his name to seize skills. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

不敗冠軍發現登記局利用他的名義奪技後，自願提供協助。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Ren sat beside the Borrow token and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Sia brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Ren. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，任坐在借用令牌旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。希雅帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。任並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "ash", "award", "boot", "chimney", "colorful". The next line held "historian", "ivory", "joyful", "keyboard", "lung". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「ash」、「award」、「boot」、「chimney」、「colorful」；下一行則有「historian」、「ivory」、「joyful」、「keyboard」、「lung」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "outer", "palm", "scout", "skate", "snap". The next line held "staff", "subtract", "tow", "yearly", "youngster". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「outer」、「palm」、「scout」、「skate」、「snap」；下一行則有「staff」、「subtract」、「tow」、「yearly」、「youngster」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Vault Behind Glass'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈玻璃後的保險庫〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 16｜The Vault Behind Glass｜玻璃後的保險庫

*英文故事正文：822詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Inside the vault Ren discovers that each stolen skill is tied to a person's identity. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任在保險庫發現每項被偷技能都與一個人的身分綁定。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Sia did not agree with everything Ren said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Ren asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，希雅並不完全同意任的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，任則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Ren sat beside the Borrow token and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Sia brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Ren. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，任坐在借用令牌旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。希雅帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。任並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "advanced", "advertise", "armed", "bulb", "chop". The next line held "comma", "fuel", "hug", "meadow", "moist". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「advanced」、「advertise」、「armed」、「bulb」、「chop」；下一行則有「comma」、「fuel」、「hug」、「meadow」、「moist」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "passion", "quote", "rotten", "separation", "spaghetti". The next line held "spill", "sting", "sum", "thirst", "thread". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「passion」、「quote」、「rotten」、「separation」、「spaghetti」；下一行則有「spill」、「sting」、「sum」、「thirst」、「thread」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Price of Every Loan'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈每次借用的代價〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Inside the vault Ren discovers that each stolen skill is tied to a person's identity.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：任在保險庫發現每項被偷技能都與一個人的身分綁定。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 17｜The Price of Every Loan｜每次借用的代價

*英文故事正文：828詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The registry offers Ren unlimited power if he accepts a contract that removes every owner's right to refuse. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

登記局承諾給任無限能力，代價是剝奪每位原主人的拒絕權。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Ren and Sia walked through Slot City with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Ren marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。任和希雅拿著新畫的圖走過技能城，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。任標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.
C. The registry offers Ren unlimited power if he accepts a contract that removes every owner's right to refuse.

**答案：C。** 核心事件：登記局承諾給任無限能力，代價是剝奪每位原主人的拒絕權。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. Sia
B. The queen of a distant planet.
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：A。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. Slot City
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：B。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. A secret order is always better than public evidence.
C. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.

**答案：C。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
B. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：A。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 18｜No to Unlimited Power｜拒絕無限力量

*英文故事正文：837詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren publicly rejects the offer and shares proof of the hidden contracts. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任公開拒絕提案，並公布隱藏契約的證據。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

'Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.' Sia said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Ren listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Ren could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。」希雅讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。任傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。任感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

There was still a distance between understanding a problem and solving it. Ren and Sia walked through Slot City with a fresh drawing, asking the same question in different places. A child noticed a mark that adults had missed. An elder remembered a change made years before. The clues did not fit together at once, but neither did they cancel each other out. Ren marked each source and refused to invent a missing answer. They returned with dirty shoes and a clearer idea of what needed a second visit. Sometimes the small witness was the one who showed the safest way forward.

**繁中**

理解問題與解決問題之間仍有距離。任和希雅拿著新畫的圖走過技能城，在不同地方問相同問題。一個孩子注意到大人忽略的記號，一位長者想起多年前的變化。線索沒有立即拼合，卻也不互相抵銷。任標註每項來源，不替空白捏造答案。他們帶著髒鞋子回來，更清楚哪些地方需要再訪。有時指向安全道路的，正是最不起眼的證人。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "almond", "attract", "clinic", "float", "follower". The next line held "inventor", "lawn", "mend", "probable", "product". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「almond」、「attract」、「clinic」、「float」、「follower」；下一行則有「inventor」、「lawn」、「mend」、「probable」、「product」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

When the plan was discussed, Sia did not agree with everything Ren said. Their argument began over a detail that seemed small, but it soon exposed a difference in what each considered fair. A volunteer offered to settle the issue by a quick vote. Ren asked to hear from the people who would bear the greatest risk first. The answer changed the proposal. Nobody enjoyed admitting they had missed something, yet the room became quieter after the correction. It was easier to make a useful choice when disagreement was allowed to stay in the open.

**繁中**

討論計畫時，希雅並不完全同意任的話。爭論始於一個看似微小的細節，卻揭露了兩人對公平的不同理解。有志工提議立刻投票解決，任則要求先聽取風險最高者的意見。回答改變了提案。沒有人喜歡承認自己漏看了什麼，但訂正後房間反而安靜。當分歧能公開存在時，人們更容易做出有用的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "receipt", "robe", "security", "shepherd", "shiny". The next line held "strip", "tank", "tender", "unite", "wagon". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「receipt」、「robe」、「security」、「shepherd」、「shiny」；下一行則有「strip」、「tank」、「tender」、「unite」、「wagon」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A City Without Shields'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈沒有護盾的城市〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

*英文故事正文：845詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The registry shuts down protective skills across Slot City, placing ordinary residents at risk. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

登記局關閉技能城的防護技能，使一般居民陷入危險。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。

**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Human Chain'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈人力接力〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. The registry shuts down protective skills across Slot City, placing ordinary residents at risk.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：登記局關閉技能城的防護技能，使一般居民陷入危險。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 20｜The Human Chain｜人力接力

*英文故事正文：848詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Players lend small skills in turns, creating a safe chain of aid without one all-powerful hero. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

玩家輪流借出小技能，建立安全互助網而非依賴全能英雄。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

A messenger reached Slot City carrying two conflicting reports. Ren read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Sia placed the Borrow token beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達技能城。任慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。希雅將借用令牌放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。任回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "chill", "comfort", "dash", "fountain", "invitation". The next line held "mathematical", "merry", "opportunity", "outline", "paradise". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「chill」、「comfort」、「dash」、「fountain」、「invitation」；下一行則有「mathematical」、「merry」、「opportunity」、「outline」、「paradise」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

'Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.' Sia said, reading from the page they had written together. The sentence sounded simple until it was tested by actual need. A stranger asked for an exception, claiming the danger was too urgent for ordinary rules. Ren listened and checked whether anyone else would pay for that exception. The answer was not comfortable. It required time, an explanation, and a promise to review the decision when new evidence appeared. Ren could feel the crowd waiting for a dramatic command. Instead, the group made a smaller choice they could explain to the people who would live with it.

**繁中**

「借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。」希雅讀出兩人一起寫下的句子。這話聽來簡單，直到真正的需求開始考驗它。一名陌生人以危險太急迫為由要求例外。任傾聽後先確認是否有其他人得替這個例外付代價。答案並不舒服：他們需要時間、說明，以及在新證據出現時重審的承諾。任感覺人群等著一道戲劇性命令，團隊卻做出較小、能向承擔後果的人解釋的選擇。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "persuade", "reduce", "scream", "similarity", "someday". The next line held "stormy", "temper", "trend", "underwear", "vest". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「persuade」、「reduce」、「scream」、「similarity」、「someday」；下一行則有「stormy」、「temper」、「trend」、「underwear」、「vest」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'The Final Match'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈最後決賽〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 21｜The Final Match｜最後決賽

*英文故事正文：830詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren enters the championship with no attack power and defeats the registry's trap through shared timing. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任以零攻擊力進入冠軍賽，靠共同計時破解登記局陷阱。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

A messenger reached Slot City carrying two conflicting reports. Ren read them slowly rather than asking everyone to trust the louder witness. Sia placed the Borrow token beside the papers and drew a line under the part they could verify. It was tempting to guess what had happened, especially when people were afraid. Yet one wrong guess could send a friend into danger. The group wrote down the times, the names, and the things nobody had seen clearly. Someone objected to wasting time. Ren replied that a moment spent checking facts was less expensive than a mistake that could not be repaired.

**繁中**

一名信使帶著兩份互相矛盾的報告抵達技能城。任慢慢讀完，沒有要大家相信聲音較大的證人。希雅將借用令牌放在紙張旁，在可以確認的地方畫線。尤其人們害怕時，猜測發生了什麼很容易；但一次猜錯就可能把朋友送入危險。團隊記下時間、姓名和沒有人看清的事。有個人抗議浪費時間。任回答，花一點時間查證，總比犯下無法補救的錯誤便宜。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "apologize", "assist", "bead", "coach", "column". The next line held "dumb", "elect", "feather", "forever", "jealous". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「apologize」、「assist」、「bead」、「coach」、「column」；下一行則有「dumb」、「elect」、「feather」、「forever」、「jealous」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The first test gave the team a little hope and one new problem. Ren insisted they record both results. Sia watched the boundary of the working area and told observers to keep their distance. The second test was slower, but it showed exactly where the old method failed. A careless account would have called the effort a complete success. They knew better. Each person signed the record only for the part personally checked, leaving space for another witness to disagree. The honest gaps in the notes were not weaknesses; they were signs that the writers understood the limits of what they knew.

**繁中**

第一次測試帶來一點希望，也帶來一個新問題。任堅持同時記下兩項結果。希雅守著工作區邊界，請旁觀者保持距離。第二次測試更慢，卻準確指出舊方法的失敗位置。草率的紀錄會把行動說成完全成功，但他們知道不能如此。每個人只為親自確認的部分簽名，也留空間讓其他證人提出異議。筆記裡誠實的空白不是弱點，而是執筆者理解自身所知有限的證明。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "kilometer", "lettuce", "lifetime", "limb", "mankind". The next line held "playful", "rug", "sausage", "scissors", "vocabulary". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「kilometer」、「lettuce」、「lifetime」、「limb」、「mankind」；下一行則有「playful」、「rug」、「sausage」、「scissors」、「vocabulary」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Name Returned'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈歸還的名字〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. Ren enters the championship with no attack power and defeats the registry's trap through shared timing.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：任以零攻擊力進入冠軍賽，靠共同計時破解登記局陷阱。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Sia

**答案：C。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Slot City
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 22｜A Name Returned｜歸還的名字

*英文故事正文：827詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Sia helps the first stolen skill owner recover legal recognition and the right to reclaim power. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

希雅協助第一位被偷技能者恢復法律身分與取回能力的權利。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。

It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. Sia helps the first stolen skill owner recover legal recognition and the right to reclaim power.
B. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：A。** 核心事件：希雅協助第一位被偷技能者恢復法律身分與取回能力的權利。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. Sia
C. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.

**答案：B。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Inside an unreachable star.
B. At a school that floated above the sun.
C. Slot City

**答案：C。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
B. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：A。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They kept copies and allowed corrections.
C. They sold it without telling the witnesses.

**答案：B。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 23｜The Registry on Trial｜登記局受審

*英文故事正文：822詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The city opens a public hearing, and borrowers testify without being forced to reveal private details. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

城市舉行公開聽證，借用者不必揭露私密細節也能作證。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

News of the plan reached the Skill Registry before sunset. The official reply was quick, polished, and strangely certain. Ren read it twice, looking for the questions it refused to answer. Sia noticed that it described a public problem as a private disagreement, although many witnesses had been affected. They did not accuse anyone merely because the answer sounded unpleasant. Instead, they requested the original documents and the name of the person responsible for storing them. A rival deserved the chance to respond to a clear question, but not the right to hide evidence behind an impressive signature.

**繁中**

日落前，計畫的消息傳到技能登記局。官方回覆快速、漂亮，而且確定得古怪。任讀了兩次，尋找其中刻意避開的問題。希雅注意到它把影響多名證人的公共問題描述成私人爭執。他們沒有因回答難聽就隨意指控，而是要求原始文件與保管人的姓名。對手有機會回答清楚的問題，卻沒有權利躲在漂亮簽名後藏匿證據。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "advantage", "brake", "capture", "continent", "dinosaur". The next line held "dragonfly", "drain", "educate", "faith", "gesture". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「advantage」、「brake」、「capture」、「continent」、「dinosaur」；下一行則有「dragonfly」、「drain」、「educate」、「faith」、「gesture」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

The discussion was interrupted by somebody who had not been invited. The visitor carried a story about the Skill Registry, but the story mixed known facts with guesses. Ren asked the visitor to separate what had been seen from what had merely been heard. Sia offered water and a chair, not a promise that every claim would be believed. A useful detail emerged only after the visitor stopped trying to sound certain. The team compared it with the earlier notes and found a new question. They could not solve it that night, so they wrote it down without pretending otherwise.

**繁中**

一位不請自來的訪客打斷討論。此人帶來關於技能登記局的故事，卻把已知事實和猜測混在一起。任請訪客分開親眼所見與道聽塗說。希雅遞上水和椅子，卻不承諾全部相信。訪客不再假裝很確定後，才說出有用細節。團隊對照早期筆記，發現新問題。當晚無法解決，他們便把問題寫下，不假裝已經找到答案。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "hallway", "immediate", "majority", "nun", "overseas". The next line held "passage", "scholar", "specific", "stiff", "tutor". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「hallway」、「immediate」、「majority」、「nun」、「overseas」；下一行則有「passage」、「scholar」、「specific」、「stiff」、「tutor」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'A Shared Skill Charter'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈技能共享憲章〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

## Chapter 24｜A Shared Skill Charter｜技能共享憲章

*英文故事正文：817詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：20個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Players vote for a transparent skill-sharing registry that anyone may leave freely. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

玩家投票支持透明的技能共享機構，任何人都能自由退出。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

Before leaving, Ren wrote one line at the bottom of the page: the day's answer could still be corrected. Sia checked that people affected by the decision could read it and reply. A messenger brought news that the next meeting would concern 'One Slot, Many Friends'. Neither knew precisely what this new matter would demand. They did know what should not be traded away to save a few hours: honest records, a safe path, and the right of someone weaker to disagree. Ren carried the unfinished notes toward Slot City, listening to the footsteps behind them and thinking of the question that morning would bring.

**繁中**

離開前，任在紙頁底下寫道：今天的答案仍然可以訂正。希雅確認受決定影響的人能讀到並回應。信使傳來消息，下次會議將處理〈一格與許多朋友〉。兩人都不確定新事件要求什麼，卻知道哪些東西不能只為節省幾小時就被交換掉：誠實紀錄、安全道路，以及弱者提出異議的權利。任帶著未完成的筆記走向技能城，聽著背後腳步聲，想著明早會出現的問題。

### 理解測驗（每題三選一）

**Q1. What new event did this chapter reveal?**

A. All the characters decided to leave without learning anything.
B. Players vote for a transparent skill-sharing registry that anyone may leave freely.
C. A spaceship moved the entire country onto the moon.

**答案：B。** 核心事件：玩家投票支持透明的技能共享機構，任何人都能自由退出。

**Q2. Who helped Ren examine the evidence?**

A. The queen of a distant planet.
B. An unnamed ghost who never appeared in the story.
C. Sia

**答案：C。** 希雅與任一起行動。

**Q3. Where did the group work on the problem?**

A. Slot City
B. Inside an unreachable star.
C. At a school that floated above the sun.

**答案：A。** 故事主要地點是技能城。

**Q4. Which rule guided the group?**

A. The strongest person can force everyone to agree.
B. Borrowing a skill requires clear consent, a time limit, and a safe way for its owner to call it back.
C. A secret order is always better than public evidence.

**答案：B。** 本章遵守的原則是：借用技能必須獲得明確同意、設定時限，並讓原主人能安全收回。

**Q5. What did they do with the written evidence?**

A. They destroyed it before anybody could read it.
B. They sold it without telling the witnesses.
C. They kept copies and allowed corrections.

**答案：C。** 正文寫到團隊保存紀錄、保留更正空間。

## Chapter 25｜One Slot, Many Friends｜一格與許多朋友

*英文故事正文：820詞｜英中配對：8組｜本章新目標詞：21個*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Ren keeps the humble Borrow skill and opens a school teaching the art of asking before taking. Ren felt the meaning of this new event before the full crowd understood it. In Slot City, each unusual sign could become a rumor by evening, and rumors were hard to pull back once they had spread. Sia stood beside the nearest witness and asked for the sequence of actions, not a dramatic conclusion. Some details contradicted what they had learned the day before. That was disappointing and useful at the same time. Ren opened a fresh page, wrote the date at the top, and decided the truth would need more than one voice.

**繁中**

任保留不起眼的借用技能，開辦一所教人先詢問再取用的學校。 群眾尚未完全理解時，任已感到這件新事的分量。在技能城，每個異常徵兆到了晚上都可能變成謠言，而謠言一旦傳開就很難收回。希雅站到最近的證人身邊，詢問行動的先後順序，而非戲劇性的結論。有些細節與昨天得到的資訊矛盾，既令人失望也很有用。任翻開新的一頁，在上方寫下日期，決定真相需要不止一個人的聲音。


**EN**

At the end of the day, Ren sat beside the Borrow token and tried to count what had genuinely changed. There was no sudden victory, but several people who had been afraid now knew whom to ask for help. Sia brought a second copy of the records and placed it out of reach of anyone who might remove the first. The plan had become less exciting and more dependable. That did not disappoint Ren. The question was never how to appear fearless. It was how to leave a safer place behind, even if the next person in charge had a different name.

**繁中**

一天結束時，任坐在借用令牌旁，試著清點真正改變了什麼。沒有突然的勝利，但原本害怕的幾個人如今知道該向誰求助。希雅帶來第二份紀錄，放到即使第一份被拿走也不會一起消失的地方。計畫變得不那麼刺激，卻更可靠。任並不失望。問題從來不是如何顯得無所畏懼，而是即使下個負責人換了名字，也能留下更安全的地方。


**EN**

While others argued over a fast solution, Sia asked who had the right to make the final decision. Ren could have answered with a name or a title. Instead, they made a list of everyone whose home, work, or safety would change. The list was longer than expected. Some people could come to a meeting; others needed a visit or a written invitation. There was no way to make a difficult choice painless, but a choice made without listening would be worse. The team planned another discussion and made sure the invitation did not require wealth or rank.

**繁中**

其他人爭論最快解法時，希雅問誰有權作最後決定。任本可回答一個姓名或頭銜，卻改列出家園、工作或安全會受到影響的所有人。清單比預期長。有些人能來開會，另一些需要拜訪或書面邀請。艱難選擇不可能毫無痛苦，但不傾聽就做決定更糟。團隊安排下一次討論，確保受邀不需要財富或階級。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "aboard", "beetle", "bet", "broadcast", "crab". The next line held "destroy", "editor", "enjoyable", "frank", "interrupt". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「aboard」、「beetle」、「bet」、「broadcast」、「crab」；下一行則有「destroy」、「editor」、「enjoyable」、「frank」、「interrupt」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

There was a practical problem behind the dramatic story. A route could look short on paper and still be impossible for a tired person to use. Ren walked it at a normal pace while Sia counted the steps and listed obstacles. At the narrowest point they stopped because the safest answer was not the fastest one. Someone suggested using the Borrow token as proof that everything was under control. Ren refused the easy claim. An impressive object could not replace a careful test, especially when strangers might have to trust it without knowing the story behind it.

**繁中**

戲劇性的故事背後有個實際問題。路線在紙上看起來很短，對疲憊的人卻可能走不了。任以正常速度走一遍，希雅數著步數並列出障礙。他們在最窄處停下，因為最安全的答案未必最快。有人提議用借用令牌證明一切都在掌握中，任拒絕了這種輕率說法。再令人印象深刻的物件，也不能取代仔細測試，尤其當陌生人必須在不知道背景時相信它。


**EN**

During a pause in the investigation, Sia unfolded an old language ledger. Five marked entries on the first line read "misery", "motel", "pine", "politics", "regional". The next line held "republic", "romantic", "salary", "studio", "stuff", "victim". None was a magic password or an answer by itself; each was a fragment of a larger history. Ren asked who had written the marks, why they had been kept, and whether they belonged to the same account. It would have been easy to declare the puzzle solved simply because the words were now visible. Instead, they copied the terms, recorded the source, and returned to the unresolved problem outside the room.

**繁中**

調查暫停時，希雅攤開一本舊語詞紀錄。第一行的五個註記寫著「misery」、「motel」、「pine」、「politics」、「regional」；下一行則有「republic」、「romantic」、「salary」、「studio」、「stuff」、「victim」。它們都不是魔法密碼，也無法單獨解答問題；每個詞只是更大段歷史的片段。任詢問誰寫下這些字、為何保存，以及它們是否屬於同一份記錄。只因文字可見就宣稱解開謎題很容易，但他們只抄下詞條、標明來源，便回到室外仍未解決的問題。


**EN**

After the meeting, Sia walked with Ren toward a quieter road. There was still a great deal to repair, and the group had not agreed on every detail. Ren admitted being afraid of failing the people who had offered their trust. Sia answered that the aim was not to become impossible to question. It was to make a plan that could be corrected before anybody's pride became everybody's danger. They checked the next appointment and promised to bring another pair of eyes. Friendship did not remove the risk. It made admitting mistakes possible, and that changed how they understood courage.

**繁中**

會議後，希雅陪任走向較安靜的道路。仍有許多事情待修，團隊也不是每項細節都同意。任坦承害怕辜負那些信任自己的人。希雅說，目標不是變成不容質疑的人，而是讓計畫能在某人的自尊危害大家之前被改正。他們確認下次約定，答應多找一雙眼睛來檢查。友情不會消除風險，但它讓認錯變得可能，也改變了他們對勇氣的理解。


**EN**

There was no perfect speech at the end. People in Slot City could question their leaders, change their minds, and share the results without waiting for a chosen hero. Ren remembered the fear of the first day and saw how many ordinary people had helped carry the work. Sia stood nearby, no longer needing to fill every silence. Tomorrow would bring repairs, meals, new arguments, and new mistakes. That was not a weakness of their victory; it was proof that life had continued. They walked home without asking the world to stop changing, ready to make another choice together.

**繁中**

結局沒有完美演說。技能城的人民終於能質疑領袖、改變想法，並分享成果，不必等待命定英雄。任想起第一天的恐懼，也看見有多少普通人共同完成這些工作。希雅站在一旁，不再需要填滿每段沉默。明天還會有修理、飯菜、新爭執與新錯誤。這不是勝利的弱點，而是生活仍在繼續的證明。他們一起走回家，不要求世界停止改變，只準備再一起做新的選擇。
